# R26 — substantial executable testability, NOT full feature completion

**50/50 modules (100.00%) meet this bounded testability standard; 0/50 (0.00%) are not established by it.**

**Fully specification-accepted modules remain 0/50 (0%).** Do not substitute the testability figure for delivery or acceptance.

At least one concrete nontrivial operational transformation, persistence operation, protocol parser/serializer, runtime integration, or executable harness with real source and an existing reproducible behavioral check. Folder presence, compilation, value types, gate/denial-only code, simulated peers and labels do not qualify. This is a reviewed lower bound on testable subsets, never full module acceptance. Test existence is not proof its complete feature works.

This is an assistant-authored review with executable path/source-hash checks, not an independent expert audit. A positive row can cover a narrow kernel in an otherwise missing feature. A negative row can contain considerable untested Android code. Existing Gradle unit targets below were invoked in prior native CI, but per-case current outcomes must be read from the exact run; a runnable target is not an inferred new pass. Android targets require the disposable-emulator opt-in described in BUILD.md.

R35 deepens again with WordDiff: word-level LCS diff over caller tokens (line-diff walk order, capped matrix) executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with SmartQuote: a deterministic typographic-quote educator with the elision narrowing declared rather than hidden, executed on host JVM. R39 also adds HyphenSoft: UAX 14 soft-hyphen stripping, break candidates in stripped coordinates and split rendering, executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with DashEducate: TeX-convention dash/ellipsis education (longest-match runs, 4+ runs untouched, ASCII de-education inverse) executed on host JVM. R40 also adds ObjReplace: U+FFFC object-placeholder bookkeeping with the partition invariant (visible chars + objects == length) executed on host JVM. R41 deepens again with LineWidth: a UAX 11 East-Asian-width subset (wide/zero/narrow, ambiguous-narrow declared, controls refused) cross-checked against held-out unicodedata, with fit() that never splits a wide character, executed on host JVM. R42 deepens again with CaseCycle: the writer change-case cycle with a stated simple title rule and the Turkish-i narrowing declared, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with BidiStrip: Trojan-Source bidi-control detection, stripping and balance audit, with stripping pinned as a user-visible choice, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with QuoteNest: email quote-depth parse/strip/runs with the > ambiguity declared rather than guessed, executed on host JVM. R45 extends DashEducate with educateOutside(text, excludedRanges) so inline code and URL spans stay byte-identical while surrounding prose receives TeX dash/ellipsis education. R45 deepens again with PieceTable: immutable append-buffer piece table with UTF-16 surrogate boundary protection, line/column indexing, and bounded undo/redo history on host JVM. R45 also adds ProseEducate: span-aware prose educator composing SmartQuote + DashEducate outside fenced code, inline backtick spans, and RFC 3986 URL spans on host JVM. R46 adds DocOutlineRender: clean-room CommonMark subset parser/renderer (headings, lists, fenced code, pipe tables, inline emphasis/links) to XSS-safe HTML5 and canonical Markdown. R46 adds TrackRevisions: OOXML w:ins / w:del tracked-changes revision model with accept/reject invariants and mat2-aligned author/timestamp anonymization. R47 adds LatexMathSvg pure-JVM kernel (office). R48 adds Epub3Package pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds RtfCleanConverter pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds OpenSearchSuggestionJson pure-JVM kernel. Exhaustive finite event words reject stale restarts; no host/hardware attestation or anonymity inference. R26 deepens with amnesiac session policy, tri-state Tor posture, USBGuard-subset policy evaluation and shuffled-pad channel-avoidance kernels. R27 deepens with FirewallPolicy (fail-closed nftables proposal gated on authority, review-only text) and UsbInventory (lsusb diff with interface-suffix arrivals) executed on host JVM. R28 deepens with ConsentLedger: scoped single-use TTL consent receipts with replay/scope/expiry refusal executed on host JVM; UsbWatchReceiver authored (detection-only, parse-gated). R32 deepens again with PinRules: device-PIN strength policy (length, runs, repeats, blocklist) with every violation reported, executed on host JVM. R36 deepens again with AttemptWindow: sliding-window attempt limiting (boundary expired, future ignored) executed on host JVM. R37 deepens again with HibpRange: HIBP k-anonymity range-response scanning (35-char suffix scan, malformed lines refused) executed on host JVM. R38 deepens again with PemBound: strict single-block RFC 7468 PEM reading (matching labels, 64-char interior lines, canonical base64 trailing bits) executed on host JVM. R38 also adds KeyCadence: an explicitly evadable in-app inter-keystroke timing classifier with published 2026 thresholds and a scope string that admits hardware-keylogger blindness, executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with JwtSplit: RFC 7515 compact-serialization splitting whose result carries signatureChecked=false constitutionally, surfaces alg:none for refusal, and refuses escaped headers rather than mis-parsing, executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with OtpauthUri: otpauth:// provisioning intake that never computes codes, refuses unknown parameters, and splits the raw label before percent-decoding (the in-round escaped-colon repair, pinned by attacks on both halves), executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with RpIdMatch: WebAuthn RP-ID shape and dot-boundary matching whose result type admits the PSL rule is NOT applied here, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with SriHash: SRI parsing and strongest-wins selection that never hashes content (digests are caller-computed), executed on host JVM. R45 deepens again with CtSctPolicy: RFC 6962 Certificate Transparency multi-operator SCT quorum evaluator and distrusted-CA root/intermediate gate (CVE-2026-113470 class). R45 also adds DmaGuardAudit: fail-closed Thunderbolt/USB4/PCIe DMA posture evaluator checking IOMMU strict translation, UEFI DMAR/IVRS pre-boot protection, and lock-state port policy. R46 deepens :core:security with MacRandomizer: RFC 7217 / Tails per-SSID HMAC-SHA256 locally administered unicast Wi-Fi MAC generator. R47 adds SecureKeypadScrambler pure-JVM kernel (privacy_sec). R47 adds ClipboardSensitiveGuard pure-JVM kernel (privacy_sec). R48 adds ScreenCaptureGuard pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds ConstantTimePrimitives pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds HkdfRfc5869 pure-JVM kernel. | `:feature:browser` | Yes — subset only | Immutable session/group/split metadata and codec only; debug remote rendering belongs to :app; session-only exact-origin one-shot permission leases (not wired to browser permission UI) R21 session-owned document consent invalidation including unsupported navigation; actual native boundary spike passed. No engine permission delegation. R22 adds explicit document suspension/revocation kernel; no real renderer lifecycle bridge. R22 exact fe8dca2 full119/0 also passed; not full-spec acceptance. R26 deepens with strict URL canonicalization, bounded host allow/block matching and private-session policy. R27 deepens with ContentBlocker: hosts + ABP-subset (|domain^, @@ exceptions) compiler with suffix decisions executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R28 deepens with CookieJar: RFC 6265-subset store/match/evict with Secure/HttpOnly/SameSite executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R31 deepens again with RobotsRules: robots.txt subset parser (groups, longest-prefix match, tie-goes-Allow) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R38 deepens again with HttpRange: RFC 9110 section 14 byte-range resolution (suffix ranges, clamping, 416 refusal when nothing satisfies) executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with Punycode: the full RFC 3492 encoder/decoder with overflow refusal and xn-- bracketing, cross-checked against the held-out Python punycode codec, executed on host JVM; IDNA mapping is declared out of scope. R40 deepens again with HtmlEntity: strict HTML character references (30-name subset, numeric forms with surrogate/C1 refusals — the cp1252 quirk is a declared narrowing) cross-checked against held-out html.unescape, executed on host JVM. R41 deepens again with DataUri: RFC 2397 decode-and-describe with the RFC default mediatype surfaced and strict base64/percent paths, cross-checked against held-out base64/urllib, executed on host JVM. | `:feature:vault` | Yes — subset only | Bounded AES256-GCM envelope with fresh96-bit nonce, authenticated header/context and bidirectional external crypto verification. R31 deepens with VaultManifest: validated item manifest (separator-free names, sha digests, canonical order-independent digest) executed on host JVM; encryption stays with VaultEnvelope. R32 deepens again with LockoutPolicy: brute-force backoff over caller instants (3 free, doubling 30s, saturation) executed on host JVM. R33 deepens again with TotpCode: RFC 6238 TOTP over caller instants (base32 secrets, ±window verify, all six RFC vectors) executed on host JVM. R35 deepens again with XorSplit: 2-of-2 XOR secret splitting (caller mask, mismatches refused) executed on host JVM. R36 deepens again with ShannonBits: Shannon entropy in bits per symbol over raw bytes executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with IbanCheck: ISO 13616 mod-97 validation with check-digit generation over a declared 12-country registry subset that refuses unknown countries instead of guessing, executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with Bech32: the BIP-173/350 polymod checksum layer (both constants, mixed-case refusal, 90-char cap) with encode/decode round-trips, wallet semantics declared out of scope, executed on host JVM. R42 deepens again with PwPolicy: password policy compliance explicitly distinct from strength (stated in scope), reporting flags without ever returning substrings, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with AgeId: age recipient/identity recognition via Bech32 plus strict 5-to-8 regrouping, zero cryptography by design, executed on host JVM. R45 deepens again with HkdfExpand: RFC 5869 HKDF-SHA256 Extract-and-Expand key derivation verified against RFC 5869 Appendix A.1-A.3 test vectors on host JVM. R46 deepens :feature:vault with PassphraseDice: EFF Diceware entropy calculator, NIST SP 800-63B scorer, and rejection-sampled unbiased index mapper. | `:feature:beam` | Yes — subset only | Real quality decisions and bounded local HTTP subset, not receiver/casting acceptance R29 deepens with ShareManifest: validated share offer (bare names, mime/sha/total caps, canonical digest, frame codec) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R33 deepens again with ChunkPlan: fixed-size byte-range planning with validated resume subtraction executed on host JVM. R38 deepens again with MagnetParse: BEP 9 magnet links (urn:btih 40-hex/32-base32 both normalized, urn:btmh sha2-256 multihash, repeatable tr/x.pe, strict percent-decode) executed on host JVM. R38 also adds Bencode: a strict BEP 3 decoder (i-0e and leading zeros refused, raw-sorted unique dict keys, full-consumption, declared Long/depth/size bounds) executed on host JVM. R41 deepens again with PiecePlan: BEP 3 piece arithmetic with the power-of-two >=16KiB convention promoted to a refusal and the sizes-sum-to-total property executed, on host JVM. R43 deepens again with TrackerTier: BEP 12 tier semantics with in-tier dedupe; the whitespace-URL hole found by first execution was repaired and the attack kept, executed on host JVM. R46 deepens :feature:beam with ChunkedTransfer: resumable SHA-256 chunked file transfer state machine with out-of-order assembly and whole-file digest verification. R47 adds NoiseFraming pure-JVM kernel (product_a). R47 adds ContentRangeHttp pure-JVM kernel (product_c). R48 adds ReedSolomonQrEcc pure-JVM kernel. R49 adds CastPrivacyExclusionGate pure-JVM kernel. | `:feature:alerts` | Yes — subset only | Bounded session-only public-notice retention, eviction, expiry and clock-independent privacy removal. R29 deepens with AlertThrottle: per-key token-bucket throttle with backwards-clock clamp and refuse-when-full table executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with QuietHours: half-open minute-of-day windows with midnight wrap, boundary countdowns and brute-force-verified overlaps; the degenerate start==end window is refused as ambiguous; executed on host JVM. R42 deepens again with SnoozeSeq: capped doubling snooze ladders with monotonicity executed as a property, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with AlertKey: canonical dedupe keys whose pipe refusal keeps keys stable forever, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with Severity: the four-level alert order where escalation only moves up (de-escalation refused as a human decision), replacing the FrameBudget kernel cut after CI exposed its name collision with the existing core:perf class, executed on host JVM. R46 deepens :feature:alerts with RateWindow: Cloudflare-style sliding-window counter rate limiter and deduplication storm suppressor. R47 adds MaintenanceSilence pure-JVM kernel (product_a). R47 adds EwmaAnomalyDetector pure-JVM kernel (product_c). R48 adds CronExpressionSchedule pure-JVM kernel. R42 deepens again with Rule3rds: thirds gridlines, power points and nearest-point snapping, geometry only, executed on host JVM. | `:feature:lens` | Yes — subset only | Finite quarter-turn/mirror/fit/crop transforms and inverse, checked with exact rational references. R30 deepens with ZoomLadder: half-stop digital-zoom ladder (ratio/nearest-index, 1x-16x clamp) executed on host JVM. R35 deepens again with EvShift: exposure-value arithmetic (EV equation plus aperture/shutter solvers and equivalent shifts; ISO outside) executed on host JVM. R36 deepens again with DofNear: thin-lens depth-of-field limits via the hyperfocal distance (far infinity as null) executed on host JVM. R37 deepens again with FovMath: rectilinear field of view plus 35mm-equivalent scaling executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with Mrz: ICAO 9303 7-3-1 check digits and TD3 line-2 structure validated against the published specimen line, with no-OCR/no-identity-claims stated in scope, executed on host JVM. R41 deepens again with Ean13: GS1 1-3 check-digit algebra (a third checksum family after MRZ 7-3-1 and Luhn) with UPC-A bridging, executed on host JVM. R34 deepens again with ZlibHead: RFC 1950 header reader (CMF/FLG, FCHECK enforced, preset dictionaries refused) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R40 deepens again with SevenZHead: the 7z 32-byte signature header with the StartHeaderCRC gate verified against held-out zlib.crc32, executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R42 deepens again with ArHead: the unix ar / Debian .deb outer structure (60-byte headers, even-offset padding, deb layout check), executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with DebControl: Debian control-paragraph fields with continuations and dot markers, complementing ArHead, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with DebVersion: the Policy 3.2.2 total order incl. the tilde rule, re-implemented independently on both sides of the mirror, executed on host JVM. R46 deepens :format:archive with TarPax: POSIX.1-2001 ustar + PAX extended-header tar reader/writer with zero-epoch reproducibility and path-traversal/symlink refusal. | `:format:ooxml` | Yes — subset only | Existing bounded literal/CSV/strict XML/ZIP/new-copy subset plus R24 exact-empty UTF8 encoder/decoder fast path. New strict JDK differential, empty input-vs-record/cell and dense32780byte frame attacks pass. Exact23e8868 original dense CSV8 and boundary27 passed unchanged5s watchdog/8s reply limits after prior deadline failures retained. Not full Office or universal timing/fidelity guarantee. R29 deepens with CsvDialect: delimiter sniffing plus quote-aware translation into the CsvTable comma grammar, executed end-to-end on host JVM against real fixtures. R39 deepens again with SerialDate: Excel 1900/1904 serial-date arithmetic keeping the Lotus phantom 1900-02-29 as serial 60 per ECMA-376, cross-checked against Python datetime, executed on host JVM. R39 also adds ColLetters: the bijective base-26 A..XFD column alphabet with the full 1..16384 bijection executed as one property, on host JVM. R39 also adds ColWidth: the ECMA-376 section 18.3.1.13 character/pixel/file-width conversions (MDW model, Excel default 8.43 chars = 64 px pinned) executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with NumFmtSections: ECMA-376 section 18.8.31 number-format structure (quote/bracket/escape-aware section splitting, color and General classification) executed on host JVM; rendering stays out of scope and LiteralXlsxImport keeps refusing custom formats. R40 also adds CellErr: the seven classic SpreadsheetML error literals with format/parse bijection and the dynamic-array-era errors refused by name, executed on host JVM. R41 deepens again with SheetRange: A1 cells and rectangular ranges with corner normalization, containment, intersection and bounding boxes over the full 16384x1048576 grid, executed on host JVM and under Gradle in CI. R41 also adds R1c1Ref: the R1C1 reference style ColLetters declared out of scope — absolute/relative/mixed forms resolving against an anchor with no grid wrapping, executed on host JVM. R42 deepens again with AbsRef: $-marker absolute/mixed references with fill-shift semantics (absolute parts pinned, no grid wrapping), executed on host JVM and under Gradle in CI. R42 also adds MergedCells: merge-region disjointness, anchors and coverage per ECMA-376 mergeCells rules, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with SheetFreeze: frozen-pane cell classification and scroll-home geometry, with the cut twips formula explicitly upheld as cut, executed on host JVM and under Gradle in CI. R44 deepens again with SheetHide: hidden row/column run arithmetic (merge, membership, next-visible) for the grid renderer, executed on host JVM and under Gradle in CI. R45 deepens again with FormulaEval: deterministic spreadsheet formula parser, topological dependency DAG evaluator (#REF!/#DIV/0!/#VALUE!/#NAME?/#NUM!/#N/A/#CYCLE!), and reactive dirty-closure calculator executed on host JVM. R45 also adds FormulaXlsx: bidirectional ECMA-376 .xlsx workbook reader/writer emitting both <f> formula expressions and pre-evaluated <v> cached values (cross-checked in Python openpyxl data_only=False and data_only=True) with zero-epoch reproducible ZIP headers. R45 also adds DocxNumbering: ECMA-376 WordprocessingML numbering.xml multi-level counter resolver (decimal, Roman, alpha, ordinal, bullet, lvlRestart, numOverride) on host JVM. R45 also adds DocxPackage: clean-room ECMA-376 .docx OPC reader/writer supporting headings, styled runs, lists, and tables while refusing word/vbaProject.bin macro smuggling. R46 expands FormulaEval from 19 to 35 pure ECMA-376 functions (adding SUMIF, COUNTIF, AVERAGEIF, IFNA, VLOOKUP, INDEX, MATCH, MEDIAN, STDEV, CONCAT, LEFT, RIGHT, MID, LEN, TRIM, UPPER, LOWER). R46 adds MultiSheetBook: multi-worksheet ECMA-376 .xlsx engine with cross-sheet Sheet1!A1 and quoted 'Q1 Sales'!B2:B10 references, cross-sheet cycle detection, and zero-epoch OPC read/write. R46 adds SheetPivot: deterministic row/column group-by pivot aggregation engine (SUM, COUNT, AVERAGE, MIN, MAX) with row, column, and grand totals. R46 adds CondFormat: spreadsheet conditional formatting rule evaluator (cell comparisons, Top-N, above-average, 2/3-stop RGB color scales, and normalized [0,1] data bars). R47 expands FormulaEval from 35 to 61 pure ECMA-376 functions (XLOOKUP, XMATCH, HLOOKUP, CHOOSE, SUMIFS, COUNTIFS, AVERAGEIFS, IFS, SWITCH, XOR, TEXTJOIN, SUBSTITUTE, FIND, SEARCH, PROPER, EXACT, REPT, VALUE, NPV, PMT, FV, PV, MOD, POWER, LOG10, LN, EXP, CEILING, FLOOR, SIGN, TRUNC). R47 adds DocxRichDocument pure-JVM kernel (office). R47 adds XlsxStyledBook pure-JVM kernel (office). R48 expands FormulaEval from 61 to 95 pure functions plus inline array literals {1,2;3,4}, lexical LET, and _xlfn./_xlws./_xlpm. normalization. R48 adds SpillSheetEngine pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds PptxSlideDeck pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds PivotCubeSummary pure-JVM kernel. R48 adds XlsxDataValidationXml pure-JVM kernel. R49 expands FormulaEval from 95 to 130 pure functions with lexical LAMBDA closures, ISOMITTED, MAP/REDUCE/SCAN/BYROW/BYCOL/MAKEARRAY, and VSTACK/HSTACK/TAKE/DROP/TOCOL/TOROW/CHOOSECOLS/CHOOSEROWS/WRAPROWS/WRAPCOLS. R49 expands SpillSheetEngine to 23 spilled dynamic-array & higher-order LAMBDA operations delegating 2D array evaluation to FormulaEval. R49 adds DrawingMlChartBuilder pure-JVM kernel. R49 adds SheetSparklineRenderer pure-JVM kernel. R49 adds SheetSolverSimplex pure-JVM kernel. R49 adds OoxmlMacroVbaInspector pure-JVM kernel. R50 expands FormulaEval from 130 to 168 pure functions with matrix algebra (MDETERM, MINVERSE, MMULT, MUNIT), FREQUENCY/TREND/GROWTH/RANK/PERCENTRANK, BIT*/DEC2*/HEX2*/BIN2*/OCT2*, CONVERT, ERF/ERFC/GAMMALN, GCD/LCM, WORKDAY, and DOLLARDE/DOLLARFR. R50 expands SpillSheetEngine from 23 to 29 spilled array operations adding MMULT, MINVERSE, MUNIT, FREQUENCY, TREND, and GROWTH. R50 adds MultiSheetBook.evaluate3d and write3d for 3D cross-sheet span formulas (SUM/AVERAGE/MIN/MAX/COUNT/PRODUCT(Start:End!Range)) while preserving single-sheet evaluate() failure contracts. R50 adds ConditionalDataBarScaleEngine pure-JVM kernel. R50 adds OoxmlSparklineSvgRenderer pure-JVM kernel. R50 adds PivotCacheXmlExporter pure-JVM kernel. R51 expands FormulaEval from 168 to 215 functions. R51 expands SpillSheetEngine from 29 to 36 spilled dynamic-array operations. R51 adds writeMultiSection and inspectSectionsAndBookmarks on DocxRichDocument. R51 adds CalcChainDependencyGraph pure-JVM kernel. R51 adds PptxMasterLayoutAnimationEngine pure-JVM kernel. R51 adds R1C1FormulaTranslator pure-JVM kernel. R51 adds StructuredTableRefEngine pure-JVM kernel. R52 expands FormulaEval from 225 to 260 pure functions. R52 expands SpillSheetEngine from 36 to 42 spilled dynamic-array operations. R52 adds writeDeckWithMediaTimings and inspectSlideMediaTimings (<p:timing>) on PptxSlideDeck. R52 adds DocxContentControlBinder pure-JVM kernel. R52 adds OoxmlSparklineEngine pure-JVM kernel. R52 adds SlideShapeSnapDistributor pure-JVM kernel. R52 adds XlsxAutoFilterEvaluator pure-JVM kernel. R53 expands FormulaEval from 260 to 300 pure functions (COMPLEX/IM*, COUP*/DURATION, BETA.DIST/TDIST/FISHER/MULTINOMIAL, HANKEL/TOEPLITZ/VANDERMONDE/KRONECKER/CONVOLVE1D/PARETOGRID). R53 expands SpillSheetEngine from 42 to 48 spilled dynamic-array operations while preserving RANDARRAY refusal. R53 adds DocxEquationOmmlBuilder pure-JVM kernel. R53 adds DocxTableLayoutGridEngine pure-JVM kernel. R53 adds PptxMasterThemeColorSchemeWriter pure-JVM kernel. R53 adds XlsxPivotCacheRecordsWriter pure-JVM kernel. R41 deepens again with PdfPageGeometry: the CropBox+Rotate composition producing the actual display surface, with /UserUnit refused rather than scaled, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with PdfPerm: Table 22 permission-bit decode/encode declared as viewer policy, never enforcement, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with ObjNum: canonical indirect-reference tokens with the 65535 free-list ceiling refused on references, executed on host JVM. R30 deepens with GifInfo: GIF87a/89a logical-screen and frame-count walker (extension skipping, trailer exactness) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R33 deepens again with BmpInspect: BMP header reader (BITMAPINFOHEADER, top-down orientation, RLE refused, no pixels decoded) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R34 deepens again with JpegSeg: JPEG marker walker with SOF0/1/2 dimensions (stops at SOS, entropy never interpreted) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R36 deepens again with TiffHead: TIFF header reader (either byte order, magic 42, even IFD offset) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R38 deepens again with WebpHead: RFC 9649 RIFF/WEBP envelope and VP8X canvas/flag decoding (minus-one 24-bit dims, 2^32-1 pixel budget) executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R39 deepens again with IcoDir: ICONDIR/ICONDIRENTRY parsing with 0-means-256 dimensions, payload-kind detection and PNG-IHDR dimension agreement enforced, executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R42 deepens again with BmpHead: standalone BMP headers (top-down heights surfaced, V4/V5 refused by biSize, 4-byte stride law), executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with ExifOrient: the orientation 1..8 table with dimension swaps and the inverse involution executed; corrupt values refuse, on host JVM. R46 deepens :format:image with PngMetadataStrip: W3C PNG chunk parser, IEEE 802.3 CRC-32 verifier, and privacy chunk scrubber removing eXIf/tEXt/zTXt/iTXt/tIME. | `:privacy` | Yes — subset only | Existing offline/no-IME compartment now discards on observed input/IME/accessibility/additional-display changes, latches until an explicit empty restart and rejects stale buttons. Native real-IME-setting test authored; current-candidate device results separate. No passive-keylogger detection or hardware/host guarantee. R31 deepens with LogScrubber: deterministic PII redaction (emails, phone runs, bearer/token secrets, device paths) with idempotence executed on host JVM against a real fixture. R32 deepens again with RetentionSweep: amnesia plumbing partitioning timestamped records into keep/purge by per-kind max ages (caller clock, unknown kinds refused), executed on host JVM. R34 deepens again with Pseudonym: HMAC-SHA256 pseudonyms with context domain separation (constant-time verify) executed on host JVM with JVM=hmac interop. R35 deepens again with FieldMask: allowlist/denylist field projection over flat records executed on host JVM. R36 deepens again with KAnon: k-anonymity auditing (equivalence groups, insertion-ordered violations) executed on host JVM. R37 deepens again with UtmStrip: known tracking-parameter stripping with fragment preservation executed on host JVM. R38 deepens again with LuhnCheck: ISO/IEC 7812 Luhn validation plus PCI DSS first6+last4 PAN masking and a free-text scrubber that leaves non-PAN digit runs alone, executed on host JVM. R39 deepens again with MacAddr: IEEE 802 U/L and I/G bit analysis across three address shapes with OUI suppressed for local addresses and redaction that keeps only the administration half; local-unicast is documented as a hint, never proof of randomization; executed on host JVM. R40 deepens again with IpRedact: strict v4/v6 parsing with /24 and /48 prefix-keeping redaction whose scope string states it reduces identifiability and is NOT anonymity, cross-checked against held-out ipaddress, executed on host JVM. R42 deepens again with GpcHeader: strict Sec-GPC and gpc.json parsing that refuses invalid values rather than coercing consent state, executed on host JVM. R43 deepens again with TimeCoarse: UTC timestamp coarsening whose scope states it reduces precision and is NOT anonymity, executed on host JVM. R44 deepens again with GpsRational: EXIF GPS rational math for the presence audit, with stripping pinned as the caller act, executed on host JVM. R45 deepens again with TailsCadence: Tails 7.15.1 freshness/emergency-advisory cadence evaluator and Tails 7.14+ 60-second hard-deadline amnesic shutdown planner on host JVM. R45 also adds OverlayTouchGuard: anti-tapjacking and screen-capture input gate combining obscured-touch flags, cross-UID overlay presence, HIDE_OVERLAY_WINDOWS, and FLAG_SECURE. R46 deepens :privacy with OnionGraterFilter: Tails onion-grater Tor ControlPort (951) session command/argument/response filter with 510 Command filtered refusal and UID STREAM scoping. R46 also adds MetadataScrub: Tails mat2-inspired OOXML/ODF ZIP metadata stripper and in-place byte-length-preserving PDF Info/XMP metadata redactor. R47 adds TorSocksIsolation pure-JVM kernel (privacy_sec). R47 adds ImuSideChannelGuard pure-JVM kernel (privacy_sec). R47 adds RamWipeSchedule pure-JVM kernel (privacy_sec). R42 deepens again with NmeaLat: the NMEA ddmm.mmmm field shape DmsFormat declared out of scope, with hemisphere signs and sub-60 minute gates, executed on host JVM. R45 deepens again with UtmGrid: WGS84 Transverse Mercator UTM forward/inverse projection with Norway 32V and Svalbard 31X..37X zone exceptions verified to <1 mm round-trip.
